Using the Kyte-Doolittle scale:
- Very hydrophobic: I (4.5), V (4.2), L (3.8), F (2.8), C (2.5), M (1.9), W (0.9), A (1.8)
- Neutral: G (-0.4), T (-0.7), S (-0.8), Y (-1.3)
- Hydrophilic: N (-3.5), Q (-3.5), D (-3.5), E (-3.5), K (-3.9), R (-4.5), H (-3.2), P (-1.6)

A TM helix typically has a window average of &gt;1.6 on the Kyte-Doolittle scale.
